08       Canggang Railway Limited   • Annual Report 2021
                                                                       CHAIRMAN’S STATEMENT
                                                                                            主席報告




                                      Liu Yongliang
                                             劉永亮
                                           Chairman
                                                主席




Dear Shareholders,                                                     各位股東：

On behalf of the Board, I hereby present the annual report of the      本人謹代表董事會呈列本集團截至2021年12月
Group for the year ended 31 December 2021.                             31日止年度的年度報告。

Overview                                                               概覽
2021 is the first year in which the Group embarked on a new            2021年 是 本 集 團 於2020年 成 功 在 聯 交 所 主 板
journey after its successful listing on the Main Board of the Stock    上市後開啟新征程的第一年。一年來，公司管

Foreign exchange risk                                                   外匯風險
The Group mainly operates in the PRC with most of the transactions      本集團的業務主要於中國經營，大部分交易以
denominated and settled in RMB. It is expected that the Group           人民幣計值及結算。除少數上市合規交易、以
will not be subjected to any material adverse effects arising           港元結算股息及活期存款外，本集團預期不會
from exchange rate fluctuation except a few listing compliance          因匯率波動而受到重大不利影響。本集團目前
transactions as well as the settlement of dividend and demand           並無採用任何外匯對沖政策。儘管如此，本集
deposits in Hong Kong dollars. The Group currently does not have        團將繼續監察外匯風險，並將於有需要時採取
any foreign currency hedging policies. Nevertheless, the Group will     適當行動。
continue to monitor foreign exchange risk exposure and will take
appropriate action when needed.

THE BOARD OF DIRECTORS                                                   董事會
Overall Management                                                       整體管理
Our Board is responsible for and has general power over the              董事會負責及擁有一般權力管理及經營業務。
management and conduct of our business. The powers and duties            董事會的權力及職責由本公司章程細則所賦
of our Board are conferred by our Articles of Association. The Board     予。董事會已將本公司業務的日常管理及營運
has delegated the day-to-day management and operations of the            授權管理層負責，本身則專注處理影響本公司
Company’s business to the management, and focuses its attention          整體策略性政策、企業管治、財務及股東的事
on matters affecting the Company’s overall strategic policies,           宜，包括審閱年度財政預算、財務報表和會計

BOARD DIVERSITY POLICY                                                   董事會多元化政策
The Company has adopted the board diversity policy (the “Board           為提高董事會的有效性，本公司已採納董事會
Diversity Policy”) which sets out the objective and approach to          多 元 化 政 策（「董 事 會 多 元 化 政 策」），當 中 載
achieve and maintain diversity on the Board in order to enhance the      列實現及維持董事會多元化的目標及方法。董
effectiveness of the Board. The Board Diversity Policy provides that     事會多元化政策規定本公司必須致力確保董事
the Company should endeavor to ensure that the Board members             會成員所擁有並就支持執行本公司業務策略所
have the appropriate balance of skills, experience and diversity         需的各項技能、經驗及多元化觀點保持適當平
of perspectives that are required to support the execution of the        衡。根據董事會多元化政策，本公司於甄選董
Company’s business strategy. Pursuant to the Board Diversity             事候選人時會考慮多項多元化因素，包括但不
Policy, selection of candidates for Directors will be based in a range   限於專業經驗、性別、年齡、文化、獨立性、
of diversity perspectives, including but not limited to professional     教育背景、知識、技能及服務年資。委任的最
experience, gender, age, culture, independence, educational              終決定將根據所選定候選人的優點及將為董事
background, knowledge, expertise and length of service. The              會作出的貢獻而作出。董事會相信，以用人唯
ultimate decision of the appointment will be based on merit and          才的準則委任董事將最能有利於本公司繼續為
the contribution which the selected candidates will bring to the         股東及其他持份者服務。
Board. The Board believes that such merit-based appointments
will best enable the Company to serve the Shareholders and other
stakeholders going forward.

appointments based on merits with reference to the Board Diversity       則，同時參考董事會多元化政策。董事會亦將

The rail freight and road freight transportation businesses are            鐵路貨運及道路貨運業務本質上屬危險業務，

RESPECT OF THE CONSOLIDATED

made reasonable judgements, estimates and assumptions based             選擇合適的會計政策及作出合理判斷、估計及
on historical experiences and various other factors, consistently       假設。綜合財務報表乃按持續經營基準編製，
with previous financial periods, as disclosed in the notes to the       並作出真實而中肯的反映。本公司獨立核數師
consolidated financial statements. The consolidated financial           的責任載於本年報第99至104頁。
statements are prepared on a going concern basis and give a true
and fair view. The responsibilities of the independent auditor of the
Company are set out on pages 99 to 104 of this annual report.

In supporting above, the finance division prepared the consolidated     為支援上述工作，財務部門已編製綜合財務報

location of trains and centralize the process of dispatch, loading,      車的過程。該系統提高服務的準繩，以助滄港
and unloading of trains. The system improves the accuracy of our         公司立即採取行動應對不幸事故。
service and allows Canggang Company to take immediate action if
unfortunate accidents happen.

follow:

     railway track and roadbed, in order to improve the reliability of        材料，令軌道更為可靠，運輸更加穩定。
     the track and stability of transportation.

     by telephones. Such a change could raise the effectiveness of            此通過縮短操作週期而提高運作效率。
     our operation by shortening the cycle time of operation.

Whistle-blowing policy                                                      舉報政策
Our group set up confidential channels to encourage employees to            本集團已設立保密渠道，鼓勵員工就任何可疑
raise their concerns regarding any suspected conflict of interest,          利益衝突、不當行為及貪污行為提出疑慮。所
misconduct and corrupt practice. All reported cases would be                有舉報個案均會迅速由獨立的內審部進行徹底
promptly and thoroughly investigated by our independent internal            調查，並連同足夠證據向董事會及審核委員會
audit department. They would then be reported to the Board and              匯報，而滄港公司的保密渠道會將舉報人匿名。
the Audit Committee if there is sufficient evidence. Canggang
Company’s confidential channels will keep the reporter anonymous.

If such report represents any criminal offences, Canggang Company           倘若報告顯示涉及任何刑事犯罪行為，滄港公
will consult with our legal advisor and report to relevant authorities if   司將會諮詢集團法律顧問並於必要時上報有關
needed.                                                                     當局。

During the year ended 31 December 2021, Canggang Company did                於截至2021年12月31日止年度，滄港公司並無
not receive any notices regarding non-compliance about corruption,          收到任何有關本集團不遵守貪污、賄賂、欺詐
bribery, fraud and money laundering of our group with relevant laws         及洗錢相關法律及法規的通知，即報告期內並
and regulations, which means there were no legal cases brought              無針對本集團的法律案件。
against the Group in the Reporting Period.

             comparison.

OPINION                                                                  意見
We have audited the consolidated financial statements of Canggang        我們已審計載於第105至179頁的滄港鐵路有限
Railway Limited (“the Company”) and its subsidiaries (“the Group”)       公 司（「貴 公 司」）及 其 附 屬 公 司（「貴 集 團」）的
set out on pages 105 to 179, which comprise the consolidated             綜合財務報表，該等財務報表包括於2021年12
statement of financial position as at 31 December 2021, the              月31日的綜合財務狀況表、截至該日止年度的
consolidated statement of profit or loss, the consolidated statement     綜合損益表、綜合損益及其他全面收益表、綜
of profit or loss and other comprehensive income, the consolidated       合權益變動表及綜合現金流量表，以及綜合財
statement of changes in equity and the consolidated statement of         務報表附註，包括主要會計政策概要。
cash flows for the year then ended and notes to the consolidated
financial statements, including a summary of significant accounting
policies.

In our opinion, the consolidated financial statements give a true and    我們認為，綜合財務報表乃根據國際會計準則
fair view of the consolidated financial position of the Group as at 31   理事會（「國際會計準則理事會」）頒佈的國際財
December 2021 and of its consolidated financial performance and          務報告準則（「國際財務報告準則」）真實而中肯
its consolidated cash flows for the year then ended in accordance        地反映 貴集團於2021年12月31日的綜合財務
with International Financial Reporting Standards (“IFRSs”) issued        狀況及截至該日止年度的綜合財務表現及綜合
by the International Accounting Standards Board (“IASB”) and             現金流量，並已遵照香港公司條例的披露規定
have been properly prepared in compliance with the disclosure            妥為擬備。

BASIS FOR OPINION                                                        意見的基礎
We conducted our audit in accordance with Hong Kong Standards            我 們 已 根 據 香 港 會 計 師 公 會（「香 港 會 計 師 公
on Auditing (“HKSAs”) issued by the Hong Kong Institute of Certified     會」）頒佈的香港審計準則（「香港審計準則」）進
Public Accountants (“HKICPA”). Our responsibilities under those          行審計。我們在該等準則下承擔的責任已在本
standards are further described in the Auditor’s responsibilities for    報告「核數師就審計綜合財務報表承擔的責任 」
the audit of the consolidated financial statements section of our        部分中作進一步闡述。根據香港會計師公會頒
report. We are independent of the Group in accordance with the           佈的專業會計師道德守則（「守則」）以及開曼群
HKICPA’s Code of Ethics for Professional Accountants (“the Code”)        島有關我們審計綜合財務報表的任何專業道德
together with any ethical requirements that are relevant to our audit    規範，我們獨立於 貴集團，並已按照該等規
of the consolidated financial statements in the Cayman Islands, and      範及守則履行我們的其他專業道德責任。我們
we have fulfilled our other ethical responsibilities in accordance       相信，我們所獲得的審計憑證能充足及適當地
with these requirements and the Code. We believe that the audit          為我們的審計意見提供基礎。
evidence we have obtained is sufficient and appropriate to provide a
basis for our opinion.

KEY AUDIT MATTER                                                         關鍵審計事項
Key audit matter is the matter that, in our professional judgement,      關鍵審計事項是根據我們的專業判斷，認為對

these consolidated financial statements.

1 CORPORATE INFORMATION                                              1 公司資料
  Canggang Railway Limited (the “Company”) was incorporated           滄 港 鐵 路 有 限 公 司（「本 公 司」）為 於2018
  in the Cayman Islands on 19 October 2018 as an exempted             年10月19日 根 據 開 曼 群 島 公 司 法 第22章
  company with limited liability under the Companies Law,             （1961年第3號法例，經綜合及修訂）在開
  Cap. 22 (Law 3 of 1961, as consolidated and revised) of the         曼群島註冊成立的獲豁免有限公司。本公
  Cayman Islands. The shares of the Company were listed on            司 股 份 於2020年10月23日 在 香 港 聯 合 交
  the Main Board of The Stock Exchange of Hong Kong Limited           易所有限公司（「聯交所」）主板上市。本公
  (the “Stock Exchange”) on 23 October 2020. The Company              司及其附屬公司的主要業務為透過在中華
  and its subsidiaries are principally engaged in the provision       人民共和國（「中國」）經營貨運鐵路及鐵路
  of rail freight transportation and related ancillary services by    調車場提供鐵路貨運及相關輔助服務。
  operating freight railway and railway yards in the People’s
  Republic of China (the “PRC”).

2 SIGNIFICANT ACCOUNTING POLICIES                                    2 主要會計政策
  (a) Statement of compliance                                         (a) 合規聲明

      includes all applicable individual International Financial          財務報告準則」）   （該統稱包括所有適
      Reporting Standards, International Accounting Standards             用的個別國際財務報告準則、國際會
      (“IASs”) and Interpretations issued by the International            計準則（「國際會計準則」）及詮釋）及
      Accounting Standards Board (the “IASB”) and the                     香港公司條例的披露規定而編製。該

      Ordinance. These financial statements also comply                   則（「上 市 規 則」）的 適 用 披 露 條 文。

      Governing the Listing of Securities on the Stock Exchange           披露。
      (the “Listing Rules”). Significant accounting policies
      adopted by the Group are disclosed below.

      The IASB has issued certain new and revised IFRSs that              國際會計準則理事會已頒佈若干於本
      are first effective or available for early adoption for the         集團的本會計期間首次生效或可供提
      current accounting period of the Group. Note 2(c) provides          早採納的新訂及經修訂國際財務報告
      information on any changes in accounting policies                   準則。附註2(c)載列有關首次應用該

2 SIGNIFICANT ACCOUNTING POLICIES                                          2 主要會計政策（續）
  (continued)
     (b) Basis of preparation of the financial statements                   (b) 財務報表的編製基準（續）
         (continued)

           The preparation of financial statements in conformity              編製符合國際財務報告準則的財務報
           with IFRSs requires management to make judgements,                 表需要管理層作出影響政策應用及所
           estimates and assumptions that affect the application of           報告資產、負債、收入及開支金額的
           policies and reported amounts of assets, liabilities, income       判斷、估計及假設。估計及相關假設
           and expenses. The estimates and associated assumptions             乃基於過往經驗及於各情況下相信屬
           are based on historical experience and various other               合理的多項其他因素，有關結果構成
           factors that are believed to be reasonable under the               對無法輕易從其他來源獲得的資產及
           circumstances, the results of which form the basis of              負債賬面值作出判斷的基準。實際結
           making the judgements about carrying values of assets              果可能有別於該等估計。
           and liabilities that are not readily apparent from other
           sources. Actual results may differ from these estimates.

           The estimates and underlying assumptions are reviewed on           估計及相關假設乃按持續基準進行審
           an ongoing basis. Revisions to accounting estimates are            閱。倘會計估計的修訂僅影響修訂估
           recognised in the period in which the estimate is revised          計的期間，則修訂乃於該期間確認，
           if the revision affects only that period, or in the period of      或倘修訂影響現時及未來期間，則於
           the revision and future periods if the revision affects both       修訂期間及未來期間確認。
           current and future periods.

           Judgements made by management in the application                   管理層於應用國際財務報告準則時所
           of IFRSs that have significant effect on the financial             作對財務報表有重大影響的判斷，以
           statements and major sources of estimation uncertainty             及估計不確定因素的主要來源於附註

           investment. Thereafter, the investment is adjusted for            的收購後變動及與投資相關的任何減

           investee’s net assets and any impairment loss relating to
           the investment (see Note 2(i)(ii)).

           the associate, the Group’s interest is reduced to nil and         其應佔權益，本集團的權益將會減至

  (h) Leased assets                                                      (h) 租賃資產

      At inception of a contract, the Group assesses whether the           本集團會在合約開始時評估有關合約
      contract is, or contains, a lease. A contract is, or contains,       是否屬於或包含租賃。倘合約給予為
      a lease if the contract conveys the right to control the use         換取代價而在一段時間內控制所識別
      of an identified asset for a period of time in exchange for          資產的使用權利，則該合約屬於或包
      consideration. Control is conveyed where the customer                含租賃。當客戶有權指示所識別資產
      has both the right to direct the use of the identified asset         的用途及自該用途獲得絕大部分經濟
      and to obtain substantially all of the economic benefits             利益時，即表示控制權已轉移。
      from that use.

               exists or may have decreased:

                   The recoverable amount of an asset is the                                 資產的可收回金額為公平
                   greater of its fair value less costs of disposal                          值減出售成本與使用價值
                   and value in use. In assessing value in use, the                          兩 者 的 較 高 者。在 評 估 使
                   estimated future cash flows are discounted to                             用 價 值 時，估 計 未 來 現 金

                   that reflects current market assessments of the                           幣時間價值及該資產特定

                   the asset. Where an asset does not generate                               現 至 其 現 值。凡 資 產 所 產
                   cash inflows largely independent of those                                 生的現金流入基本上並非

           other and are not offset. Current tax assets are offset                倘本公司或本集團有法定強制執行權

  (r)   Provisions and contingent liabilities                             (r)    撥備及或然負債

        Provisions are recognized when the Group has a legal                     撥備乃於本集團因過往事件而產生
        or constructive obligation arising as a result of a past                 法律或推定責任，而可能導致需以經
        event, it is probable that an outflow of economic benefits               濟利益流出清償責任，並能作出可靠
        will be required to settle the obligation and a reliable                 估計時確認。倘金錢的時間價值屬重
        estimate can be made. Where the time value of money is                   大，撥備乃按預期清償責任的開支的
        material, provisions are stated at the present value of the              現值列賬。
        expenditure expected to settle the obligation.

        Where it is not probable that an outflow of economic                     倘不大可能需要流出經濟利益或未能
        benefits will be required, or the amount cannot be                       可靠估計金額，有關責任會披露為或
        estimated reliably, the obligation is disclosed as a                     然負債，除非流出經濟利益的可能性
        contingent liability, unless the probability of outflow of               極微，則作別論。僅由於一項或多項
        economic benefits is remote. Possible obligations, whose                 未來事項發生或不發生而確認是否存
        existence will only be confirmed by the occurrence or non-               在的可能責任亦會披露為或然負債，
        occurrence of one or more future events are also disclosed               惟流出經濟利益的可能性極低者則除
        as contingent liabilities unless the probability of outflow of           外。
        economic benefits is remote.

        Where some or all of the expenditure required to settle                  倘預期結算撥備所需之部分或全數開
        a provision is expected to be reimbursed by another                      支由另一方償付，則就任何大致肯定
        party, a separate asset is recognized for any expected                   的預期償付款項確認一項單獨資產。
        reimbursement that would be virtually certain. The amount                僅可就償付款項確認撥備之賬面值。
        recognized for the reimbursement is limited to the carrying
        amount of the provision.

  (s)   Revenue and other income                                          (s)    收益及其他收入

        Income is classified by the Group as revenue when it                     本集團將於其於日常業務過程中提供
        arises from the provision of services in the ordinary course             服務所產生的收益分類為收入。
        of the Group’s business.

        Revenue is recognized when control over a service is                     收益在產品或服務的控制權轉移至客
        transferred to the customer at the amount of promised                    戶時，按本集團預期有權收取的承諾
        consideration to which the Group is expected to be                       代價金額確認，惟不包括代表第三方
        entitled, excluding those amounts collected on behalf of                 收取的款項。收益不包括增值稅或其
        third parties. Revenue excludes value added tax or other                 他銷售稅，並經扣除任何貿易折扣。
        sales taxes and is after deduction of any trade discounts.

        Where the contract contains a financing component which                  倘合約包含為客戶提供超過12個月重
        provides a significant financing benefit to the customer                 大融資利益的融資部分，則收益按應
        for more than 12 months, revenue is measured at the                      收金額的現值計量，並使用與客戶的
        present value of the amount receivable, discounted                       單獨融資交易中反映的貼現率貼現，
        using the discount rate that would be reflected in a                     而利息收入按實際利率法單獨累計。
        separate financing transaction with the customer, and                    倘合約包含為本集團提供重大融資利
        interest income is accrued separately under the effective                益的融資部分，則根據該合約確認的
        interest method. Where the contract contains a financing                 收益包括按實際利率法就合約負債所
        component which provides a significant financing benefit                 產生的利息開支。本集團利用國際財
        to the Group, revenue recognized under that contract                     務報告準則第15號所述的實際權宜方
        includes the interest expense accreted on the contract                   法，於融資期限為12個月或以下的情
        liability under the effective interest method. The Group                 況下不會就重大融資部分的任何影響
        takes advantage of the practical expedient in IFRS 15                    調整代價。
        and does not adjust the consideration for any effects of a
        significant financing component if the period of financing is
        12 months or less.

           estimated useful lives and residual values, if any, of the        產的估計可使用年期及剩餘價值（如
           property, plant and equipment and right-of-use assets             有），以釐定將於任何報告期內入賬
           regularly in order to determine the amount of depreciation        的折舊開支金額。可使用年期及剩餘
           expense to be recorded during any reporting period. The           價 值（如 有）乃 根 據 對 同 類 資 產 的 過

           are based on the historical experience with similar assets        何配置的預期變動而釐定。倘與過往

     (c) Expected credit losses for trade receivables                     (c) 貿易應收款項的預期信貸虧損

           The credit loss allowance for trade receivables are based         貿易應收款項的信貸虧損撥備乃基於
           on assumptions about the expected loss rates. The                 對預期虧損率的假設而定。於各報告
           Group uses judgement in making these assumptions and              期末，本集團會於作出該等假設及挑
           selecting the inputs to the impairment calculation, based         選輸入數據計算減值時，根據本集團
           on the Group’s past history, existing market conditions           的過往記錄、現時市場狀況及前瞻性
           as well as forward looking estimates at the end of                估計作出判斷。該等假設及估計的變
           each reporting period. Changes in these assumptions               動可能會對評估結果造成重大影響，

      are to safeguard the Group’s ability to continue as a               集團持續經營業務的能力，使其可透

      by pricing products and services commensurately with                股東帶來回報並惠及其他持份者。
      the level of risk and by securing access to finance at a
      reasonable cost.

      its capital structure to maintain a balance between the             架構，以在較高股東回報（可能伴隨
      higher shareholders returns that might be possible with             較高借貸水平）與穩健的資本狀況所
      higher levels of borrowings and the advantages and                  帶來的優勢及保障之間取得平衡，並
      security afforded by a sound capital position, and makes            依照經濟狀況的變化對資本架構作出

     Exposure to credit, liquidity and interest rate risks arises in the      本集團在日常業務過程中面對信貸、流動
     normal course of the Group’s business.                                   資金及利率風險。

     The Group’s exposure to these risks and the financial risk               本集團所面對的該等風險及本集團用以管

     manage these risks are described below.                                  述如下。

     (a) Credit risk                                                          (a) 信貸風險

           Credit risk refers to the risk that a counterparty will default      信貸風險指對手方不履行合約責任導
           on its contractual obligations resulting in a financial loss to      致本集團蒙受財務損失的風險。本集
           the Group. The Group’s credit risk is primarily attributable         團的信貸風險主要來自貿易應收款
           to trade receivables. The Group’s exposure to credit                 項。本集團就現金及現金等值項目和
           risk arising from cash and cash equivalents and bills                應收票據所承受的信貸風險有限，原
           receivable is limited because the counterparties are banks           因是對手方為本集團管理層給予高信
           and financial institutions with high credit ratings assigned         貸評級的銀行及金融機構，故本集團
           by the management of the Group, for which the Group                  認為其信貸風險偏低。
           considers to have low credit risk.

           The Group does not provide any guarantees which would                本集團不會提供將令本集團面對信貸
           expose the Group to credit risk.                                     風險的任何擔保。

           Trade receivables                                                    貿易應收款項

           The Group’s exposure to credit risk is influenced mainly by          本集團面對的信貸風險主要受各客戶
           the individual characteristics of each customer rather than          （而非客戶經營所在的行業或國家）的
           the industry or country in which the customers operate and           個別情況所影響，因此，重大集中信
           therefore significant concentrations of credit risk primarily        貸風險主要於本集團與個別客戶有重
           arise when the Group has significant exposure to individual          大業務往來時產生。於報告期末，貿

           Individual credit evaluations are performed on all                   本集團會對所有要求超過一定金額信
           customers requiring credit over a certain amount. These              貸的客戶進行個別信貸評估。該等評
           evaluations focus on the customer’s past history of making           估集中於客戶過往支付到期款項的記
           payments when due and current ability to pay, and take               錄及現時的付款能力，並考慮客戶及
           into account information specific to the customer as well            與客戶經營所在地的經濟環境有關的
           as pertaining to the economic environment in which the               特定資料。貿易應收款項於開票日期
           customer operates. Trade receivables are due within 30               起計30天內到期。本集團一般不向客
           days from the date of billing. Normally, the Group does not          戶收取抵押品。
           obtain collateral from customers.

           The Group measures loss allowances for trade receivables             本集團按相等於全期預期信貸虧損的
           and contract assets at an amount equal to lifetime ECLs,             金額計量貿易應收款項及合約資產虧
           which is calculated using a provision matrix. As the                 損撥備，有關金額乃使用撥備矩陣計
           Group’s historical credit loss experience does not indicate          算得出。由於本集團過往信貸虧損經

  (d) Currency risk                                                     (d) 貨幣風險

      The Group is exposed to currency risk primarily through             本集團所面對的貨幣風險主要來自以
      receivables, payables and cash balances that are                    外幣（即與交易相關業務的功能貨幣

      are primarily HK$.


      The Group ensures that the net exposure is kept to an               本集團確保透過於必要時以即期匯率

     (a) The impact of COVID-19 pandemic                                  (a) COVID-19疫情的影響

           The COVID-19 outbreak since early 2020 has significantly          自2020年初以來爆發COVID-19對全
           affected entities and economic activities globally and            球企業及經濟活動均造成重大影響，
           brought additional uncertainty to the Group’s operating.          對本集團的經營亦帶來額外不明朗因
           The Group had been closely monitoring the impact on the           素。本集團一直密切關注疫情對集團
           developments on the Group’s businesses and had put in             業務發展造成的影響，亦已制定應急
           place contingency measures, which includes assessing              措施，包括評估對客戶的影響及與主
           the impact on customers and liaising with key customers,          要客戶聯繫，並開拓更多銷售渠道。
           and developing more sales channels. Up to the date of this        截至本報告日期，管理層並無發現任
           report, management has not identified any areas that could        何 會 對 本 集 團 於2021年12月31日 的
           have a material impact on the financial performance or            財務表現或狀況產生重大影響之處。
           position of the Group as at 31 December 2021.

           Nevertheless, as COVID-19 continues to evolve, it is              然 而，隨 著COVID-19持 續 演 變，目
           challenging at this moment to predict the full extent and         前要全面預測疫情對業務和經濟的
           duration of its impact to the business and the economy.           影響程度及持續時間極具挑戰性。本
           The Group will continue to take measures, including but           集 團 將 繼 續 採 取 包 括（但 不 限 於）控
           not limited to, controlling its operating costs, monitoring       制經營成本、監控現金流、密切注視
           its cash flows, paying close attention to the development         COVID-19的發展對其業務營運的影
           of the COVID-19 to its operations and continuously                響並持續評估疫情對本集團財務報表
           assessing its impact to the Group’s financial statements,         的影響、適當調整業務計劃等措施，
           adequately adjusting its business plans, to protect the           以在此挑戰重重的營商環境中保障本
